### [1] [BlockMGARD: Accelerating Adaptive Scientific Data Reduction with Region-of-Interest Error Control on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00205)
*Yanliang Li,Qian Gong,Qing Liu,Jaemoon Lee,Norbert Podhorszki,Scott Klasky,Xin Liang,Jieyang Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BlockMGARD是一种支持ROI的自适应GPU有损压缩器，相比MGARD-X实现最高4.2倍和9.1倍的压缩/解压吞吐量提升，以及在ROI感知误差控制下8.63倍的压缩比提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据规模不断增长使得有损压缩变得至关重要，但现有基于变换的压缩器（如MGARD）采用多级分解虽能实现高压缩比，却在GPU架构上映射效率低下，亟需GPU友好的高效压缩方案。

Method: 提出BlockMGARD框架，包含四项创新：(1)利用GPU片上内存和常量查找表的In-cache Block分解以加速分解过程；(2)结合In-cache Block和全局分解的混合层次结构以平衡速度与压缩比；(3)支持细粒度ROI误差控制的端到端流水线以实现特征保留；(4)在四款GPU上实现了近理想线性扩展的评估。

Result: 相比MGARD-X，BlockMGARD实现了最高4.2倍压缩吞吐量和9.1倍解压吞吐量提升；在ROI感知误差控制下，压缩比最高提升8.63倍；I/O开销最高降低1.58倍，且在四款GPU上展现出近理想线性扩展性。

Conclusion: BlockMGARD成功解决了传统基于变换的压缩器在GPU架构上的映射难题，通过In-cache Block分解和混合层次设计实现了高效的科学数据有损压缩，为大规模科学数据的存储和传输提供了实用化解决方案。

Abstract: The growing scale of scientific data makes lossy compression essential for reducing data volume under controllable error. Transformation-based compressors using multilevel decomposition, such as MGARD, achieve strong compression ratios but map poorly to GPU architectures. We propose BlockMGARD, an adaptive, Region-of-Interest (ROI)-supported GPU lossy compressor, with four contributions: (1) an In-cache Block decomposition leveraging GPU on-chip memory and constant lookup tables to accelerate decomposition; (2) a hybrid hierarchy combining In-cache Block and global decomposition to balance speed and compression ratio; (3) an end-to-end pipeline with fine-grained ROI error control for feature preservation; and (4) an evaluation against state-of-the-art methods on five real-world datasets. Compared to MGARD-X, BlockMGARD achieves up to 4.2x and 9.1x higher compression and decompression throughput, and up to 8.63x higher compression ratio than uniform-tolerance baselines under ROI-aware error control. Across four GPUs, BlockMGARD achieves near-ideal linear scaling and up to 1.58x I/O cost reduction over MGARD-X.

</details>


### [2] [Operation-Type-Aware Client Routing for Leader-Based Consensus Datastores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00392)
*Sri Saran Balaji Vellore Rajakumar,James Thompson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对基于领导者的共识数据存储，操作感知的客户端路由策略通过将写操作固定到领导者、将读操作分发到健康读池，在正常状态下显著降低延迟并提升吞吐量，在节点静默降级时效果更为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于领导者的共识数据存储（如etcd、ZooKeeper）面临两个相互竞争的路由目标：负载均匀分布在成员之间，以及将操作路由到协议角色匹配的成员。当前etcd客户端使用gRPC的round_robin负载均衡器，将读写操作均匀分布到集群成员，导致写操作需要额外的转发跳数，而线性一致性读取未充分利用轻量级领导者确认机制。

Method: 设计操作感知的客户端路由策略：将写操作固定到领导者节点，将读操作分发到健康的读池成员。当检测到跟随者节点延迟异常时，将其从读池中移除。该方法在etcd（Raft协议）和ZooKeeper（ZAB协议）两种不同实现上进行了验证。

Result: 在3节点etcd集群、80/20读写混合负载下：稳定状态下，写P50延迟降低29%，吞吐量提升9%；当跟随者静默降级时，读P99降低64%，写P99降低74%，吞吐量提升89%。ZooKeeper实验呈现相同趋势，证明该策略的有效性源于基于领导者的共识结构而非特定系统实现。

Conclusion: 操作感知的路由策略能有效解决共识数据存储的路由矛盾，且该结论具有跨系统的通用性。主要障碍在于领导者确认发生在成员之间，客户端只能观察到混合延迟信号而非直接的协调开销，这阻碍了策略的自适应发现。

Abstract: Leader-based consensus datastores (etcd, ZooKeeper) face two competing routing goals: spread load evenly across members, and route operations to the member whose protocol role matches the operation. Writes must commit through the leader, so sending them elsewhere adds a forwarding hop. Linearizable reads need only a lightweight leader confirmation before any member can serve them locally. The upstream etcd client uses gRPC's round_robin balancer, distributing reads and writes uniformly across cluster members. An operation-aware client resolves this by pinning writes to the leader and distributing reads across the healthy read pool. In steady state on a 3-node etcd cluster (80/20 read/write mix, 5 trials), this lowers write P50 by 29% and raises throughput by 9%. When a follower degrades silently, the operation-aware client detects the latency shift and removes it from the read pool, cutting read P99 by 64%, write P99 by 74%, and raising throughput by 89%. The same routing rule applied to ZooKeeper (ZAB protocol, different implementation) points in the same direction, showing that the result follows from leader-based consensus structure rather than one system's implementation. The key obstacle to discovering this policy adaptively is that the leader confirmation round-trip occurs between cluster members, so the client sees only a blended latency signal rather than the decisive coordination cost directly.

</details>


### [3] [Client-side transparent caching for remote ROOT data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00400)
*Dmytro Kovalskyi,Jan Eysermans,Mariarosaria D'Alfonso,Christoph Paus*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出uCache（xrd-ucache），一种作为XRootD客户端插件实现的透明客户端缓存，无需服务器端部署或分析代码修改即可显著加速高能物理分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理分析经常处理相同数据以改进算法和测试新想法，随着数据越来越多地从远程存储读取，每次迭代都受网络延迟、带宽和共享存储吞吐量的影响，在负载下这些因素变化很大。

Method: 实现了一个透明的客户端缓存（uCache），作为XRootD客户端插件，使用分析机器上的本地存储作为网络和内存之间的缓存层。缓存只存储分析实际读取的数据，并可以将缓存数据重建为分支对齐、重新压缩的形式，从而消除后续遍历的大部分输入/输出和解压缩成本。

Result: 使用Analysis Grand Challenge顶夸克对分析和公开的CMS Open Data（使用zlib和LZMA压缩）进行基准测试。填充缓存与直接读取相比几乎没有开销。后续遍历中，字节缓存快1.6-8.8倍，重新压缩缓存快2.1-15.7倍。对于典型分析，1TB缓存足以处理10-20TB的数据集。当远程数据源负载重或地理位置较远时，改进最大。

Conclusion: uCache可以有效解决高能物理分析中的远程数据访问问题，显著提高分析效率，特别是在远程数据源负载重或地理位置远的情况下效果最佳。

Abstract: High-energy physics analyses often process the same data as physicists refine algorithms and test new ideas. With data increasingly read from remote storage, each iteration is subject to network latency and depends on network bandwidth and shared-storage throughput, which can vary substantially under load. We present uCache (xrd-ucache), a transparent client-side cache implemented as an XRootD client plugin that requires neither server-side deployment nor changes to analysis code. It uses local storage on the analysis machine as a cache layer between the network and memory. The cache stores only the data actually read by an analysis. It can also rebuild cached data into a branch-aligned, recompressed form, eliminating most of the input/output and decompression costs of subsequent passes. We benchmark the cache using the Analysis Grand Challenge top quark pair analysis on public CMS Open Data compressed with zlib and LZMA. Filling the cache adds essentially no overhead compared with a direct read. Subsequent passes are 1.6-8.8 times faster from the byte cache and 2.1-15.7 times faster from the recompressed cache. For a typical analysis, a 1 TB cache suffices for datasets of 10-20 TB. The largest improvements occur when the remote data source is heavily loaded or geographically distant.

</details>


### [4] [DRLM: Deep Reinforcement Learning-Based LLM Query Orchestration in Edge Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00442)
*Reza Farahani,Zoha Azimi Ourimi,Mario Colosi,Lauri Loven,Christian Timmerer,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DRLM是一个基于深度强化学习的边缘LLM查询编排框架，通过集成质量估计器和延迟预测器，实现了边缘环境下异构设备和多样化模型的高效查询调度，显著降低了推理延迟和排队延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务日益处理具有不同延迟、准确性和资源需求的异构查询。边缘部署虽然能减少响应时间，但设备异构性以及模型系列、参数规模和量化级别的多样性使得高效LLM查询编排变得困难。现有方法难以有效应对这种复杂的异构环境。

Method: DRLM集成了两个轻量级预测器：(i)类别条件质量估计器，将查询映射到语义类别并推断模型性能；(ii)特征驱动延迟预测器，估计跨模型-设备配置的推理时间。这些预测与系统状态结合，输入到因子化近端策略优化(PPO)智能体，执行状态感知的编排决策。研究还构建了大规模基准数据集，包含223,835个测量值，涵盖1258个查询、6个查询类别、8个模型系列（32个部署实例）、5个量化级别和异构边缘设备。

Result: 在64节点边缘集群上的评估显示，与三个基线和两个最先进方法相比，DRLM将推理延迟降低多达51%，排队延迟降低多达67%，同时最多仅产生8%的准确率损失。在增加的工作负载下，延迟改善高达61.4%，证明了编排的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: DRLM成功解决了边缘环境中LLM查询编排的挑战，通过深度强化学习和轻量级预测器的结合，实现了异构设备和多样化模型下的高效查询调度，为边缘LLM服务部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) services increasingly process heterogeneous queries with diverse latency, accuracy, and resource requirements. While edge deployment reduces response time, the heterogeneity of devices and the diversity of model families, parameter scales, and quantization levels make efficient LLM query orchestration challenging. This paper introduces DRLM, a Deep Reinforcement Learning-based LLM query orchestration framework in edge environments. DRLM integrates two lightweight predictors: (i) a class-conditioned quality estimator that maps queries to semantic categories and infers model performance, and (ii) a feature-driven latency predictor that estimates inference time across model-device configurations. These predictions, combined with system state, feed a factorized Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent that performs state-aware orchestration decisions. To enable data-driven orchestration, we construct a large-scale benchmarking dataset with 223 835 measurements spanning 1258 queries, 6 query classes, 8 model families (32 deployed instances), 5 quantization levels, and heterogeneous edge devices. Evaluation on a 64-node edge cluster and comparison with three baselines and two state-of-the-art methods show that DRLM reduces inference latency by up to 51% and queuing delay by up to 67 %, while incurring at most 8% accuracy loss. It improves latency under increasing workloads up to 61.4%, demonstrating robust and stable orchestration.

</details>


### [5] [Comparison of Algebraic Block Multi-Coloring and Leiden Methods for Parallel Preconditioning in the ICCG Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00561)
*Tomohiro Suzuki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了将Leiden社区发现方法应用于ICCG方法的不完全Cholesky预处理并行化，实验表明使用常数Potts模型的Leiden方法可以达到与经过优化的ABMC方法相当的性能，且无需预先指定块数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完全Cholesky共轭梯度(ICCG)方法中，前向和后向替换存在顺序依赖性，这构成了多核环境中并行化的主要瓶颈。现有的代数块多着色(ABMC)方法虽然通过块着色实现了并行性和数据局部性，但需要预先指定块的数量作为输入参数，这在实际应用中存在局限性。

Method: 本研究将Leiden社区发现方法作为ICCG方法并行预处理的替代阻塞方法。Leiden方法通过最大化图分区质量函数来自动生成反映矩阵结构的块，无需预先指定块数量。研究实现了使用模块度和常数Potts模型作为质量函数的Leiden方法，对稀疏矩阵的邻接图进行分区，并将结果块用于并行预处理。在八个对称正定矩阵上与ABMC方法进行性能比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用常数Potts模型的Leiden方法在迭代次数、执行时间和L2缓存效率方面，能够达到与经过优化的块数量配置的ABMC方法相当的性能水平。

Conclusion: Leiden方法可以作为ABMC方法的有效替代方案，用于ICCG方法的并行预处理，其优势在于无需预先指定块数量即可自动生成反映矩阵结构的块，同时保持与优化配置的ABMC方法相当的计算性能。

Abstract: In the application of incomplete Cholesky preconditioning to the incomplete Cholesky-conjugate gradient (ICCG) method, forward and backward substitutions exhibit sequential dependencies that constitute a major bottleneck for parallelization in multicore environments. To alleviate this bottleneck, the algebraic block multi-coloring (ABMC) method achieves both parallelism and data locality through block-wise coloring. However, ABMC requires the number of blocks to be specified as an input parameter in advance. This study evaluates the Leiden method as an alternative blocking approach for parallel preconditioning in the ICCG method. As a community detection technique that maximizes a quality function for graph partitioning, the Leiden method automatically generates blocks that reflect the matrix structure without requiring the number of blocks a priori. We partition the adjacency graphs of sparse matrices using the Leiden method and utilize the resulting blocks for parallel preconditioning. We implement the Leiden method using modularity and the constant Potts model as quality functions and compare its performance with that of the ABMC method in terms of the number of iterations, execution time, and L2 cache efficiency across eight symmetric positive definite matrices. The experimental results demonstrate that the Leiden method with the constant Potts model achieves performance comparable to that of the ABMC method configured with an optimized number of blocks.

</details>


### [6] [Characterizing the Scalability and Performance of Large-Scale AI Training Under Multi-Tenancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00817)
*Jacopo Raffi,Thomas Pasquali,Lorenzo Piarulli,Filippo Spiga,Marco Faltelli,Andreas Herten,Domenico Siracusa,Daniele De Sensi,Flavio Vella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在多个超级计算机集群（包括Alps、Leonardo、LUMI等）上对分布式AI训练进行大规模性能表征，评估了五种并行策略在高达2400个GPU上的扩展性、通信开销以及多租户并发执行下的干扰行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代HPC系统上表征AI工作负载性能需要理解其独立扩展性以及并发执行时的行为，然而并行策略、网络拥塞、计算能力和互连技术之间的相互作用仍缺乏深入理解。

Method: 设计了AI模型基准测试套件，在多个超算集群（Alps、Leonardo、LUMI、JUPITER、NVL72 GB300、DGX A100）上评估五种不同的并行策略；量化不同互连技术下的通信开销；通过设计现实噪声模型研究多任务并发训练的相互干扰。

Result: 在高达2400个GPU规模下，系统量化了scale-up、scale-out和机架级配置下不同分配策略的性能表现，揭示了通信开销对不同互连技术的影响，以及多任务并发执行时的干扰特征。

Conclusion: 提供了分布式AI训练扩展性和执行效率的系统性表征，为现实多租户场景下的性能行为提供了关键见解，有助于理解和优化大规模AI训练的性能表现。

Abstract: Characterising AI workload performance on modern HPC systems requires understanding both their scalability in isolation and their behaviour under concurrent execution. However, the interplay among parallelisation strategies, network congestion, compute capability, and interconnect technologies remains poorly understood. This work investigates the performance and scalability of AI models up to 2400 GPUs. We quantify the communication overheads and their impact across different interconnects by evaluating scale-up, scale-out, and rack-scale configurations under multiple allocation schemes. Finally, we study how multiple concurrent training jobs interfere with each other by designing a realistic noise model. We design a benchmark suite of AI models to evaluate the performance of five distinct parallelisation strategies across different supercomputing clusters, including Alps, Leonardo, LUMI, JUPITER, NVL72 GB300, and DGX A100. Our work provides a systematic characterization of the scalability and execution efficiency of distributed AI training, while offering key insights into performance behavior under realistic multi-tenant scenarios.

</details>


### [7] [Update for Decisions, Not Freshness: Goal-Oriented Status Updating and Selective Offloading at the Network Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01082)
*Jianpeng Qi,Qiyang Zhang,Chao Liu,Jing Sun,Yimei Liu,Yanwei Yu,Yingjie Wang,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CoSMO框架，通过协作式事件驱动强化学习协调语义状态管理和选择性卸载，在边缘-云协作环境中平均提升准时完成率18.6%-21.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云协作计算环境中，边缘节点需在本地执行、转发至远程服务节点或拒绝任务之间做出决策。边缘节点直接观测本地状态，但仅通过间歇刷新的缓存获取服务节点状态，导致状态更新和任务控制形成部分可观测下的异步闭环。现有的基于信息新鲜度（AoI）的方案无法直接量化更新对后续任务决策的影响。

Method: 提出CoSMO（语义状态管理与卸载协同设计）框架，采用协作式事件驱动强化学习：1）服务节点端使用循环半马尔可夫双深度Q网络（Double DQN）智能体联合选择发送/不发送决策及下一决策间隔；2）边缘节点端采用任务终止离策略价值学习智能体，基于本地观测和过时远程语义进行层次化门控-路由决策；3）两智能体维护独立观测和价值目标，但共享已实现任务效用流，无需集中式执行。

Result: 在评估的工作负载族中，CoSMO相对最佳竞争方法的准时完成率平均提升18.6%-21.2%；在三个严格过载点上的容量感知决策准确率平均提升17.6%-17.9%。

Conclusion: CoSMO框架成功解决了部分可观测下的异步决策问题，通过协同设计语义状态管理和任务卸载，显著提升了任务完成性能和决策准确性，为边缘-云协作环境下的状态管理与资源调度提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In an edge--cloud collaborative edge-computing environment, an edge node (EN) must decide whether each user task should be executed locally, forwarded to a remote service (or cloud) node (SN), or rejected. The EN observes its local state directly but receives the SN state only through an intermittently refreshed cache. Status updating and task control therefore form an asynchronous closed loop under partial observability. Freshness-driven schemes, including those based on Age of Information (AoI), do not directly value an update by its effect on subsequent task decisions. We propose CoSMO (Co-design of Semantic-state Management and Offloading), a cooperative event-driven reinforcement learning (RL) framework that coordinates semantic status management and selective offloading through realized task utility. CoSMO learns a compact representation of the heterogeneous SN service state. At the SN, a recurrent semi-Markov double deep Q-network (Double DQN) agent jointly selects send/no-send and the next decision interval. At the EN, a task-terminal off-policy value-learning agent makes hierarchical gate--route decisions from local observations and stale remote semantics. The agents maintain separate observations and value targets but share the same realized task-utility stream, without centralized execution. Across the evaluated workload families, CoSMO's reported relative improvement in on-time completion rate over the best-performing competing method averages 18.6%--21.2%. For capacity-aware decision accuracy across the three strict-overload points, the corresponding reported gains average 17.6%--$17.9%.

</details>


### [8] [Breaking Cycles for Scalable Fair Ordering in Blockchain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00837)
*Jinchun He,Wangjie Qiu,Yizhong Liu,Shengda Zhuo,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashOrder通过局部化循环歧义，实现了比现有系统Themis高10.5倍、比Rashnu高4.8倍的吞吐量，同时在对抗性攻击下显著降低了排序偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链系统中交易顺序直接决定财务结果，不公平排序已导致超过6.86亿美元的损失。现有公平排序协议在争议或对抗操作下，因Condorcet循环被迫进行全局强连通分量凝聚，导致延迟、批次粗糙和扩展性失败。

Method: FlashOrder是一种确定性公平排序引擎：将成对偏好嵌入一维规范位置，使用分割超图聚类邻近交易，执行分层集群间和集群内序列化，用局部化排序和聚合替代批处理级SCC凝聚。

Result: 在libhotstuff原型上评估，吞吐量比Themis高10.5倍、比Rashnu高4.8倍；对抗模拟中最大排名位移减少88.7%；Condorcet攻击下平均吞吐量分别比Themis和Rashnu高12.0倍和9.7倍。

Conclusion: 局部化循环歧义能在大幅提高吞吐量的同时实现更强的公平性，为区块链公平排序提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: In blockchain systems, transaction order directly determines financial outcomes: unfair ordering enables front-running and sandwich attacks that have extracted over \$686M from Ethereum users. Current fair-ordering protocols aggregate pairwise receive-order evidence from replicas. Under contention or adversarial manipulation, however, Condorcet cycles force them into global strongly connected component (SCC) condensation, causing delays, coarse batches, and scaling failures.
  We present FlashOrder, a deterministic fair-ordering engine that localizes cyclic ambiguity before it propagates across the batch. FlashOrder embeds pairwise preferences into one-dimensional canonical positions, clusters nearby transactions with a partition hypergraph, and performs hierarchical inter- and intra-cluster serialization, replacing batch-wide SCC condensation with localized sorting and aggregation. Evaluated against Themis (CCS '23) and Rashnu (VLDB '24) on a libhotstuff-based prototype, FlashOrder achieves up to 10.5$\times$ higher throughput than Themis and 4.8$\times$ higher than Rashnu, with the latency gap widening as network scales. In controlled adversarial simulation, it reduces maximum rank displacement by 88.7\%, and under Condorcet attacks it sustains 12.0$\times$ and 9.7$\times$ higher throughput than Themis and Rashnu on average. These results show that localizing cyclic ambiguity yields stronger fairness at substantially higher throughput.

</details>


### [9] [Shared-Memory Range-Tiled CDF Sort for Small-Range Integer Keys on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00843)
*Kento Ando,Kaito Takase,Noriyuki Fujimoto,Koichi Wada*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了RT-CDF算法，一种基于计数排序的GPU整数排序方法，在小到中等范围的整数排序上实现了最高4.39倍加速，但受限于直方图构建成本，在大范围场景下性能不如基线算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU整数排序方法在已知整数范围的场景下仍有优化空间，特别是针对不稳定排序和计数排序方法的性能提升存在需求。研究旨在利用已知值范围信息，开发更高效的GPU整数排序算法。

Method: 提出Range-Tiled CDF sort（RT-CDF）算法：将可能的值范围划分为适合共享内存的小区间（tiles）；对每个tile构建直方图，计算前缀和作为局部CDF；直接从局部CDF生成输出数组。对比基线包括CUB DeviceRadixSort（限制处理比特范围以利用已知范围大小R）、Ref-H-P sort以及Kolonias等人的算法实现。

Result: 在RTX 4090上测试，范围大小R=2^7到2^18，输入规模n=10^6到10^9，均匀分布、正态分布和全等输入条件下：RT-CDF在小到中等范围广泛条件下优于所有基线，最高加速比4.39倍；但在R=2^18时，对所有评估的输入规模和分布，至少一个基线优于RT-CDF，表明直方图构建成本限制了RT-CDF在大范围场景的适用性。

Conclusion: RT-CDF算法通过值范围分块和局部CDF构建，在小到中等范围的整数排序任务中显著优于现有方法，证明了利用已知范围信息的有效性；然而，直方图构建开销成为算法扩展到更大范围的关键瓶颈，为未来研究指明了优化方向。

Abstract: We study unstable integer sorting on GPUs for arrays whose elements lie in a known integer range. Focusing on counting-sort-based methods that determine the output interval of each value from its frequency and the prefix sums of the frequencies, we propose and evaluate Range-Tiled CDF sort (RT-CDF), which partitions the possible value range into small intervals, called tiles, that fit in shared memory. For each tile, RT-CDF constructs a histogram, computes its prefix sum as a local CDF, and directly generates the output array from the local CDF. We compare RT-CDF against three baselines: CUB DeviceRadixSort, whose processed bit range is restricted to $[0,\lceil\log_2 R\rceil)$ to exploit the known range size $R$; Ref-H-P sort; and an implementation based on the algorithm of Kolonias et al. Experiments on an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 with range sizes from $R=2^7$ to $2^{18}$, input sizes from $n=10^6$ to $10^9$, and uniformly distributed, normally distributed, and all-equal inputs show that RT-CDF outperforms the baselines over a broad set of conditions for small to medium ranges, achieving a maximum speedup of 4.39 over the fastest baseline. For $R=2^{18}$, however, at least one baseline outperforms RT-CDF for every evaluated input size and input distribution, showing that the cost of histogram construction limits the applicability of RT-CDF to larger ranges.

</details>


### [10] [Prediction-Robust Service Deployment with Capacity-Aware Edge Admission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00877)
*Hailiang Zhao,Ziqi Wang,Yifei Zhang,Mingyi Liu,Xinkui Zhao,Kingsum Chow,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPSUM及其弹性变体CAPSUM-E，一种面向边缘平台容量感知的在线服务准入策略。在常见TTL成本模型下，CAPSUM通过尺寸缩放盈亏测试、利用率相关影子价格和证据密度驱逐机制，相比最佳基线适配器降低33.7-45.5%的归一化成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘平台通过在用户附近实例化可执行服务以降低请求服务成本，但每个实例会产生一次性部署成本且仅在有限的TTL内有效。这导致在线决策问题具有预测敏感性和容量耦合特性：过于乐观的预测会浪费部署成本，而延迟决策则会错失其旨在服务的突发流量。如何在预测误差和容量约束下做出最优部署决策是核心挑战。

Method: 1. 将节点-服务追踪问题规约为变价Bahncard实例，使CAPSUM-E继承PFSUM的预测误差依赖比率，实现2/(1+β)一致性和1/β鲁棒性；2. 设计重定向感知变体保持相同本地部署调度；3. 对于有限容量节点，CAPSUM结合尺寸缩放盈亏测试、利用率相关影子价格和证据密度驱逐机制；4. 实现精确的本地离线动态规划算法。

Result: 在容量可行性、尺度不变性和弹性配置下与CAPSUM-E的精确一致性方面得到理论证明。实验涵盖控制预测误差、三种合成需求模式、Globus Compute追踪上的因果预测器以及1024节点和10000服务的联合扩展。在常见模型下，CAPSUM相比最佳源派生适配器在合成模式上降低33.7-42.9%归一化成本，在采样追踪上降低45.5%成本。

Conclusion: CAPSUM成功解决了边缘平台服务部署中预测敏感性和容量耦合的在线决策问题，通过将问题规约为经典算法问题并设计容量感知机制，在理论和实验层面均展示了显著的性能提升，为边缘计算环境下的资源优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Edge platforms instantiate executable services close to users to reduce request-serving cost, but each instance incurs a one-time deployment cost and remains useful only for a finite time-to-live (TTL). The resulting online decision is both prediction-sensitive and capacity-coupled: an optimistic forecast can waste deployment cost, whereas a delayed decision misses the burst it is intended to serve. We study this problem under a common TTL cost model and propose CAPSUM, a capacity-aware admission policy with an elastic specialization, CAPSUM-E. In the local elastic setting, every node-service trace is exactly a variable-price Bahncard instance. This reduction lets CAPSUM-E inherit PFSUM's tight prediction-error-dependent ratio, including $2/(1+β)$ consistency and $1/β$ robustness for $β>0$. A redirect-aware variant preserves the same local deployment schedule. For finite-capacity nodes, CAPSUM combines size-scaled break-even tests, a utilization-dependent shadow price, and evidence-density eviction; we prove capacity feasibility, scale invariance, and exact agreement with CAPSUM-E under an elastic configuration. We implement an exact local offline dynamic program and compare against direct common-model baselines and documented source-derived adapters for EDP-A, OREO, and uEDC-L. Experiments cover controlled prediction error, three synthetic demand regimes, a causal predictor on a public Globus Compute trace, and joint scaling to 1,024 nodes and 10,000 services. Under the common model, CAPSUM reduces normalized cost by 33.7-42.9% relative to the best source-derived adapter across the synthetic regimes and by 45.5% on the sampled trace.

</details>


### [11] [AInfer-PD: Communication-Safe In-Place Prefill-Decode Multiplexing for Distributed MoE Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00993)
*Guowei Wang,Chaokun Yang,Zhenxuan Pan,Yuhong Guo,Minghua Zhu,Zhechuan Zhang,Shuo Wan,Xiaowei Zhu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了AInfer-PD系统，通过协调P/D集合通信顺序和隔离DeepEP路径状态，实现了分布式MoE推理中Prefill和Decode的高效并发执行，显著降低了强化学习rollout推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模强化学习的agentic RL场景中，rollout推理往往主导墙钟时间。异步轨迹使得Prefill(P)和Decode(D)在rollout过程中持续共存，而非一次性事件。在共享加速器上，这种持续的P/D共存会导致Prefill干扰延迟敏感的Decode操作，延长rollout完成时间。现有的P/D分离方案需要独立设备池和KV-cache传输，而现有的原地复用设计缺乏大规模MoE部署所需的通信隔离机制。

Method: 提出AInfer-PD系统，扩展了原地P/D复用以支持分布式MoE rollout。关键技术包括：(1) 跨rank协调P/D集合通信顺序，避免交叉ADP/ATP集合通信的冲突；(2) 为DeepEP的P和D路径提供独立的通信状态，消除共享可变协议状态的问题。该设计在保持共享模型权重和KV存储的同时，协调同一设备上的P和 D操作。

Result: 在单节点Prefill密集型工作负载上，相较于禁用P/D复用的AInfer引擎，固定工作负载rollout完成时间减少7.1-22.5%；相较于SGLang减少24.8-32.9%。在双节点设置上，减少幅度分别为18.0-35.3%和18.3-31.8%。消融实验显示，细粒度边界相较全epoch异步入队可进一步减少8.6-19.8%的完成时间。

Conclusion: AInfer-PD成功解决了分布式MoE rollout中P/D并发的通信隔离问题，在保持共享资源优势的同时实现了高效的P/D并发执行，为大规模强化学习推理提供了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Rollout inference often dominates the wall-clock time of large-scale reinforcement learning (RL). In agentic RL, each trajectory alternates between model generation and environment interaction over multiple turns. Asynchronous trajectories consequently introduce new prefill (P) work while other trajectories remain in decode (D), making P/D coexistence a persistent property of the rollout rather than a one-time prompt-ingestion event.
  On shared accelerators, persistent P/D coexistence can make prefill interfere with latency-sensitive decode and prolong rollout completion. P/D disaggregation avoids this co-location but requires separate device pools and KV-cache transfers. In-place multiplexing retains shared devices and KV state, but existing designs lack the communication isolation needed for large MoE deployments that combine attention TP/DP with distributed expert execution. In practical implementations, P and D can issue intersecting collectives in inconsistent cross-rank orders; DeepEP's P and D paths also share mutable protocol state.
  We present AInfer-PD, which extends in-place P/D multiplexing to distributed MoE rollouts. AInfer-PD coordinates P/D collective order across ranks and gives the two DeepEP paths independent communication state, making crossed ADP/ATP and DeepEP paths safe for concurrent P/D execution. The design retains shared model weights and KV storage while coordinating P and D on the same devices. Across repeated single-node prefill-intensive workloads, AInfer-PD reduces fixed-workload rollout completion time by 7.1-22.5% relative to the same AInfer engine with P/D multiplexing disabled and by 24.8-32.9% relative to SGLang. On two nodes, the reductions are 18.0-35.3% and 18.3-31.8%, respectively. In a same-engine ablation, fine-grained boundaries reduce completion time by a further 8.6-19.8% over whole-epoch asynchronous enqueue.

</details>


### [12] [MakoXC: Rearchitecting DFT Exchange-Correlation with Matrix-Aligned and Knowledge-Organized Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01025)
*Haozhi Han,Fusong Ju,Jing Bai,Ruge Zhang,Xiang Zhao,Liang Yuan,Yunquan Zhang,Ting Cao,Liu Yunxin,Yifeng Chen,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MakoXC是一个针对密度泛函理论中交换相关计算优化的新型引擎，通过重构近视性诱导的稀疏性，在AI加速器上实现了平均67.8倍的速度提升，成功将千原子级泛素体系的DFT计算时间压缩至五分钟以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论（DFT）在材料科学和药物发现中不可或缺，但交换相关（XC）评估由于其三次方复杂度而成为主要计算瓶颈。现有线性缩放方法虽然利用电子近视性降低了渐近复杂度，但产生了不规则的稀疏工作负载，这些负载隐藏了隐式稀疏性并阻碍了对现代AI加速器的高效利用。

Method: MakoXC通过协同设计三项关键技术重构XC评估：(1) 矩阵对齐单元将近视性诱导的交互重组为密集、加速器对齐的数据簇；(2) 稀疏引导激活将更深的隐式稀疏性转化为数值正确的结构化执行，实现实际的线性缩放；(3) 内核融合流水线将碎片化工作负载整合为统一的高密度计算执行路径，充分释放加速器吞吐量。

Result: 评估结果表明，MakoXC相比标准XC评估平均加速67.8倍，相比最先进的线性缩放方法加速4.7倍。集成到生产级商业DFT软件包后，MakoXC在64个GPU上成功扩展XC评估至泛素体系（1,231个原子，def2-SVP基组），使端到端DFT计算在五分钟内完成。

Conclusion: 通过将XC评估重构为统一的结构化计算，MakoXC展示了科学工作负载如何在AI加速器上实现真正的低复杂度并最大化并行效率，为高性能科学计算提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Density Functional Theory (DFT) is indispensable for materials science and drug discovery, yet the exchange--correlation (XC) evaluation remains a major bottleneck due to its cubic scaling. Although linear-scaling methods exploit electronic nearsightedness to reduce asymptotic complexity, they produce irregular sparse workloads that hide implicit sparsity and prevent efficient use of modern AI accelerators. We present MakoXC, a modular matrix-aligned XC evaluation engine that rearchitects nearsightedness-induced sparsity into regular, accelerator-friendly computations. MakoXC co-designs three key techniques: (1) Matrix-Aligned Cells reorganize nearsightedness-induced interactions into dense, accelerator-aligned data clusters; (2) Sparsity-Guided Activation translates deeper implicit sparsity into numerically correct structured execution for practical linear scaling; and (3) Kernel-Fused Pipeline consolidates fragmented workloads into a unified, compute-intensive execution path that fully unleashes accelerator throughput. Extensive evaluations show that MakoXC achieves average speedups of 67.8$\times$ speedup over standard XC evaluation and 4.7$\times$ over state-of-the-art linear-scaling methods. When integrated into a production-grade commercial DFT package, MakoXC scales XC evaluation to ubiquitin (1,231 atoms, def2-SVP) on 64 GPUs, enabling the end-to-end DFT calculation to complete in under five minutes. By restructuring XC evaluation into a unified, structured computation, MakoXC demonstrates how scientific workloads can achieve genuine low complexity while maximizing parallel efficiency on AI accelerators.

</details>


### [13] [Just Talk Once: Communication-Efficient Split Federated LLM Fine-Tuning on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01457)
*Jiaxiang Geng,Xianhao Chen,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出L-shaped SFT和one-shot SFT两种框架，通过消除双向通信瓶颈和允许客户端离线，显著降低了边缘设备上大语言模型微调的通信成本和客户端在线时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的联邦学习在边缘设备上面临内存、计算、带宽和连接性限制。现有的分割联邦微调(SFT)虽然提高了客户端效率，但需要跨分割接口的逐步双向通信循环，并强制客户端在整个训练过程中持续参与，成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 1. 提出L-shaped SFT框架：利用现代LLM中的权重共享机制，使服务器端的隐藏激活可以直接使用目标嵌入进行监督，从而在服务器端计算训练损失，无需将服务器输出返回给客户端；2. 基于L-shaped SFT提出one-shot SFT：客户端只需上传一次激活即可离线，服务器在缓存的表示上继续优化。

Result: 在包含商用智能手机和NVIDIA开发板的异构边缘客户端真实系统测试平台上实现，实验表明所提方案相比现有SFT基准方法显著降低了通信成本和客户端在线时间。

Conclusion: L-shaped SFT和one-shot SFT成功消除了传统SFT中的双向通信瓶颈和持续客户端参与需求，为边缘设备上的LLM微调提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) fine-tuning is increasingly shifting toward data generated on edge devices, where memory, computation, bandwidth, and connectivity constraints make conventional federated learning difficult to sustain. Split federated fine-tuning (SFT) improves client-side efficiency by offloading most model parameters and computation to the server but requires step-by-step bidirectional communication loop across the split interface and forces continuous client involvement throughout training. In this paper, we present L-shaped SFT, a split fine-tuning framework that removes this bidirectional bottleneck. Our key insight is that weight tying in modern LLMs enables server-side hidden activations to be directly supervised using target embeddings, allowing the training loss to be computed on the server without returning server outputs to the client. To further eliminate the need for continuous client participation, based on L-shaped SFT, we introduce one-shot SFT, in which clients upload activations once and then go offline while the server continues optimization over cached representations. We implement our design in a real system testbed with heterogeneous edge clients, including commercial smartphones and NVIDIA developer boards. Experiments demonstrate that our schemes significantly reduce communication costs and client online time compared with existing SFT baselines.

</details>


### [14] [Faster Convergence of Multidimensional Approximate Agreement via Smallest Enclosing Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01490)
*Darya Melnyk*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了BallMidpoint协议，将多维近似一致问题的收缩率从约0.935提升至约0.707，显著缩小了与下界的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优收缩率为√(7/8)≈0.935，远大于一维情况的最优值1/2，本文旨在缩小这一差距，设计收缩率更接近下界的多维协议。

Method: 提出BallMidpoint协议：采用局部安全区域的最小包围球的中点作为输出点，满足凸有效性要求。

Result: 在同步与异步模型下均实现了1/√2≈0.707的收缩率，达到最优容错能力；该速率对所提算法是紧的。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了多维近似一致协议的收缩率更接近已知下界而非上限1，显著优于此前最优方案。

Abstract: This work considers the multidimensional approximate agreement problem. In this problem, $n$ parties in a distributed system, up to $t$ of which may be corrupted by a Byzantine adversary, need to output vectors that are close to each other and that lie inside the convex hull of all non-corrupted input vectors. We assume that nodes communicate in a fully-connected authenticated network and analyze synchronous and asynchronous communication models. The focus of this work is on the contraction factor of approximate agreement protocols.
  The first multidimensional approximate agreement protocols had a contraction rate of $1-1/n$(VG, PODC'13) and $\sqrt[d]{1/2}$(MH, STOC'13). While a rate below $1$ is sufficient for convergence, it is not sufficient for practical applications. To date, the best known convergence rate of approximate agreement algorithms is $\sqrt{7/8}\approx0.935$ (FN, DISC'18), which is achieved through the MidExtremes protocol. This stands in contrast to the lower bound on the convergence rate in the $1$-dimensional setting, which is $1/2$.
  In this work, we propose BallMidpoint - a novel approximate agreement protocol with a contraction rate of $1/\sqrt{2}\approx 0.707$ in the synchronous and the asynchronous communication models. This algorithm satisfies the optimal resilience under convex validity. The presented contraction rate is achieved by choosing the midpoint of the smallest enclosing ball of the so-called local safe areas, and it is tight for the presented algorithms. Similar to (FN, DISC'18), our algorithm is coordinate-free, and the point inside the safe area can be computed efficiently. This work presents the first multidimensional approximate agreement protocol where the convergence rate is closer to the best known lower bound rather than the upper bound of $1$.

</details>

### [15] [mzCache: On-Device LLM Memory Management under Multitasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01338)
*Hongseung Yu,Minsung Kim,Jongseok Park,Kyunghan Lee*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 针对移动设备多任务环境下的内存压力问题，提出了mzCache系统，通过弹性内存驱逐和CPU-GPU并发恢复机制，实现零等待的LLM推理，将首个令牌时间（TTFT）降低2.1-5.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备在动态多任务环境下频繁切换应用，导致操作系统强制驱逐LLM内存（模型权重和KV缓存），当新推理请求到达时需要通过慢速存储读取或重新计算来恢复内存，严重影响响应速度。

Method: 提出mzCache系统，采用面向恢复的内存管理策略：将LLM内存划分为细粒度共享缓冲区以支持部分驱逐和跨处理器并发访问；利用移动SoC的统一内存特性实现GPU零等待推理与CPU端并发恢复；采用混合交换和后向驱逐策略确保低延迟恢复。

Result: 在llama.cpp上实现并部署为Android应用，mzCache相比基于存储的部分卸载方案，将Time-to-First-Token降低2.1-5.5倍，在真实多任务场景中证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: mzCache通过专门的内存管理机制有效解决了移动设备多任务环境下的LLM推理延迟问题，实现了高效、响应迅速的设备端LLM推理系统。

Abstract: On-device mobile Large Language Model (LLM) inference is gaining significant attention. However, mobile devices operate in highly dynamic multitasking environments where users frequently switch between applications. This creates memory pressure, forcing LLM memory (model weights and KV cache) to be evicted by the operating system. When a new inference request arrives, the inference system must restore the evicted memory through slow storage reads or recompute the entire KV cache, severely degrading responsiveness. To address this, we present mzCache, an on-device LLM inference system with specialized memory management for multitasking environments. Under unpredictable memory pressure, mzCache elastically evicts LLM memory and leverages the unified memory of mobile SoCs to enable zero-wait inference on the GPU with concurrent CPU-side restoration. mzCache realizes this through restoration-oriented memory management: LLM memory is partitioned into fine-grained shared buffers to enable partial eviction and restoration with concurrent cross-processor access, while hybrid swap and backward-out eviction policies ensure low-latency restoration from any eviction state. Implemented on llama.cpp and deployed as an Android application, mzCache achieves 2.1-5.5$\times$ reduction in Time-to-First-Token compared to storage-backed partial offload and demonstrates its effectiveness in real multitasking scenarios.

</details>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [16] [DynaNDE: Dynamic Near-Data Expert Scheduling for Batched MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00407)
*Xiaoyang Lu,Belthangady Akash Vi Narayana Pai,Xian-He Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaNDE框架，通过动态近数据专家调度和NPU-NDP协同执行加速批量MoE推理，在prefill和decoding阶段分别实现2.6×和2.2×的平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在NPU系统上部署时存在严重的数据移动开销；现有的NPU-NDP MoE系统未能充分考虑硬件异构性、动态专家级并发性和批量推理时的时序专家重用特性。

Method: 提出DynaNDE动态近数据专家调度框架：1）建立分析性能模型，刻画硬件异构性、数据移动成本和NPU-NDP协同执行中的通信-计算重叠；2）基于该模型确定每层的专家调度策略，同时考虑专家级并发性；3）引入重用感知运行时，避免专家参数在NPU内存中的冗余移动。

Result: 实验结果表明，DynaNDE相比最先进的NPU-NDP MoE服务框架实现了显著的吞吐量提升，在prefill阶段平均加速2.6×，在decoding阶段平均加速2.2×。

Conclusion: 通过充分挖掘NPU-NDP协同执行潜力并考虑硬件异构性、专家并发性和参数重用，DynaNDE有效解决了MoE推理中的数据移动瓶颈，为大规模MoE模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable efficient scaling of large language model (LLM) inference but suffer from substantial data-movement overhead when deployed on neural processing unit (NPU)-based systems. Near-Data Processing (NDP) provides a promising way to mitigate this bottleneck via cooperative NPU-NDP execution. However, existing NPU-NDP MoE systems do not fully account for hardware heterogeneity, dynamic expert-level concurrency, and temporal expert reuse during batched inference. This paper presents DynaNDE, a dynamic near-data expert scheduling framework that exploits NPU-NDP collaboration to accelerate batched MoE inference. DynaNDE introduces an analytical performance model that captures hardware heterogeneity, data-movement costs, and communication-computation overlap in cooperative NPU-NDP execution. Guided by this model, DynaNDE determines per-layer expert scheduling across the NPU and NDP while accounting for expert-level concurrency. DynaNDE also incorporates a reuse-aware runtime that avoids redundant parameter movement when experts reside in NPU memory. Experimental results show that DynaNDE achieves substantial throughput improvements over the state-of-the-art NPU-NDP MoE serving framework, with average speedups of 2.6$\times$ and 2.2$\times$ for the prefill and decoding stages, respectively.

</details>


### [17] [VARA: A Voltage-Aware ReRAM-Based Accelerator for Energy-Efficient Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00421)
*Peng Dang,Yintao He,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出VARA加速器，通过电压感知训练(VAT)增强激活稀疏性，并结合协同零激活重排序(CAR)方案优化ReRAM内存计算架构，实现60.12%的系统能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReRAM加速器主要关注权重优化，而忽视了激活分布对计算能耗的影响，导致激活稀疏性的节能潜力未被充分挖掘。由于ReRAM交叉开关在模拟域执行矩阵向量乘法，其计算能耗高度依赖权重和激活分布，激活优化成为关键。

Method: 提出两个核心方法：(1)电压感知训练(VAT)算法，将预设阈值融入激活函数，引导激活分布趋向零值以增强稀疏性；(2)协同零激活重排序(CAR)方案，基于共零相关性聚类激活维度，重排激活矩阵和对应权重，将分散的零激活整合为连续零值区域，最大化交叉开关级计算跳过的收益。

Result: 实验结果表明，在仅存在边缘精度损失的情况下，VARA相比基线平均降低总系统能耗60.12%，系统平均能效提升2.68倍，优于现有针对稀疏激活优化的最先进加速器。

Conclusion: VARA通过联合优化激活稀疏性和重排序策略，有效挖掘了ReRAM加速器中激活稀疏性的节能潜力，为ReRAM内存计算架构提供了有效的能耗优化方案。

Abstract: ReRAM-based in-memory computing (IMC) architectures are widely regarded as a promising approach to alleviating the computational bottleneck of conventional architectures. Since ReRAM crossbars perform matrix-vector multiplication (MVM) in the analog domain, their computational energy consumption is highly dependent on weight and activation distributions. However, most existing ReRAM accelerators focus primarily on weight optimization while paying limited attention to the impact of activations on computational energy consumption, leaving the energy-saving potential of activation sparsity largely underexploited. In this paper, we propose a voltage-aware ReRAM-based accelerator (VARA), along with its accompanying design methodology. Specifically, we first introduce a voltage-aware training (VAT) algorithm that incorporates a preset threshold into the activation function to steer the activation distribution toward zero values, thereby enhancing activation sparsity. Building upon this, we further propose a co-zero activation reordering (CAR) scheme for crossbar-level computation skipping. CAR clusters activation dimensions based on their co-zero correlations and consistently reorders both the activation matrix and its corresponding weights. This process consolidates scattered zero activations into contiguous zero-valued regions to maximize the benefits of crossbar-level computation skipping. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that, with only marginal accuracy loss, VARA reduces the average total system energy consumption by 60.12\% and improves the average system energy efficiency by 2.68$\times$ compared to the baseline, outperforming existing state-of-the-art accelerators for sparse-activation optimization.

</details>


### [18] [LLM Inference on IMC-NoC Architecture with Balanced Dataflow and Fine-Grained Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00857)
*Yimin Wang,Yue Jiet Chong,Xuanyao Fong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LEAP架构，通过IMC、NMC和INC的统一架构解决LLM推理中的内存墙和通信瓶颈问题，相比商用GPU实现1.52倍吞吐量提升和24.91倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理服务对内存带宽、计算密度和通信效率提出了前所未有的需求。虽然内存内计算(IMC)能缓解内存墙问题，但LLM的异构数据动态性需要互补资源处理运行时生成的中间数据。此外，LLM的海量参数需要扩展型架构，而片上数据移动成为主要性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出软硬件协同设计框架：(1)硬件层面设计LEAP可扩展架构，集成IMC PE处理静态权重、NMC PE处理动态数据、INC处理部分结果归约；(2)软件层面提出针对LLM服务关键指标(吞吐量、延迟)优化的分区、映射和调度框架；(3)采用预填充-解码分离方法，动态重配置PE组织以应对两个阶段不同的计算强度。

Result: 相比商用GPU平台，所提架构实现≥1.52倍吞吐量提升和24.91倍能效提升。

Conclusion: 通过将分布式计算、内存和通信统一为无缝的处理-通信架构，LEAP有效解决了LLM推理中的内存带宽瓶颈、动态数据处理和片上通信开销等关键挑战，为LLM推理服务提供了高效的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: LLM inference has become an essential service, yet it imposes unprecedented demands on memory bandwidth, computational density, and communication efficiency. While IMC is a promising solution to the memory wall issue, the heterogeneous data dynamicity of LLM requires complementary resources to handle intermediate data generated during run-time. Furthermore, the massive number of parameters in LLM necessitates scale-up architectures where on-chip data movement is often the primary performance bottleneck. This article presents a hardware-software co-design framework that unifies distributed compute, memory, and communication into a seamless processing-communication fabric. On the hardware side, we propose a scalable architecture, named LEAP, that integrates IMC PE, NMC PE, and INC. This allows each hardware layer to execute specialized tasks: IMC for static weights, NMC for dynamic data, and INC for partial result reduction. On the software side, we introduce a partitioning, mapping, and scheduling framework optimized for key metrics in LLM serving, including throughput and latency. To address the distinct computational intensities of the prefill and decode phases, we present a prefill-decode disaggregation approach that dynamically reconfigures PE organizations to maximize resource utilization. Compared to commercial GPU platforms, the proposed architecture provides a throughput and an energy efficiency improvement of $\geq{}1.52\times$ and $24.91\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [19] [Hardware Acceleration of Block-Diffusion LLM for Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01084)
*Wei-Hsing Huang,Kiseok Lee,Ming-Yen Lee,Weiyu Sun,Cheng-Jhih Shih,Gayatri Tanksali,Arpit Khandelwal,Pin-Jun Chen,Yingyan Celine Lin,Shimeng Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出WIFiV-LPDDR、BRQ-KV和DAT-FFN协同设计方案，解决边缘推理中权重流量无法分摊和扩散模型KV缓存/FFN权重重复流式传输的问题，在保持精度损失小于1个百分点的前提下实现约4倍能效提升和约3-4倍延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单流边缘推理无法在请求间分摊权重流量开销；全注意力扩散LLM每步需重新计算整个序列，原生块扩散虽使已完成块可缓存，但细化过程仍需流式传输前缀KV和FFN权重，造成显著的内存带宽瓶颈和能耗开销。

Method: 协同设计三方面：(1) WIFiV-LPDDR系统支持精度标记读取；(2) BRQ-KV方案采用低秩加INT8残差的前缀表示，支持查询依赖的逐条目精度；(3) DAT-FFN方案采用漂移映射规范替换、相邻阶段校正低位增量或缓存状态传递，同时保持激活值不量化。两方案均映射到输入固定型混合精度脉动阵列。

Result: 在1.5B/7B模型及Jetson类平台上，全栈方案实现算术平均能耗降低因子3.79x/3.96x，算术平均延迟加速2.88x/4.44x；所有压缩模型基准测试分数较基线下降均小于1个绝对百分点。

Conclusion: 所提协同设计方案有效解决了边缘扩散模型推理中的内存带宽瓶颈，在几乎不损失模型精度的条件下显著提升了能效和推理速度，为边缘端大模型部署提供了可行的优化路径。

Abstract: Single-stream (batch-one) edge inference cannot amortize weight traffic across requests. Full-attention diffusion LLMs recompute the entire sequence at every step; native block diffusion makes completed blocks immutable and exactly cacheable, yet refinement still streams prefix KV and FFN weights. We co-design WIFiV-LPDDR, a wide-I/O LPDDR system for precision-tagged reads, BRQ-KV for a canonical low-rank-plus-INT8-residual prefix with query-dependent per-entry precision, and DAT-FFN for drift-mapped canonical replacement, adjacent-stage-corrected low-bit delta, or cached-state carry while keeping live activations unquantized. Both map to an input-stationary mixed-precision systolic array. For the evaluated 1.5B/7B models on modeled Jetson-class platforms, the full stack provides arithmetic-mean energy-reduction factors of 3.79x/3.96x and arithmetic-mean latency speedups of 2.88x/4.44x at the reported DAT-FFN settings; every corresponding compressed model-benchmark score drops by less than one absolute percentage point from its baseline.

</details>


### [20] [Performance Characterization of SPEC CPU 2026 on AMD EPYC 9755 Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01527)
*Kunal Kashyap,Rajiv Ramanathan,Shayantika Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次对SPEC CPU 2026基准测试套件进行微架构级性能特征分析，使用AMD EPYC "Zen 5"处理器，通过多维度方法识别出三种不同的工作负载集群，并揭示了仅在系统满载时才出现的规模化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPEC CPU 2026是自2017年以来行业标准CPU基准测试套件的首次重大更新。本研究旨在为下一代数据中心处理器提供架构研究和工作负载驱动设计决策的实证基础，填补新基准套件微架构特征分析的空白。

Method: 采用多维度分析方法，涵盖流水线效率、控制流行为、缓存层次压力和指令混合，分析SPECrate和SPECspeed套件。引入规模分析方法，比较单副本运行与全系统运行行为，以暴露传统特征分析无法发现的系统级瓶颈。

Result: 识别出三种不同的行为集群：（1）前端控制流主导型工作负载，主要考验分支预测器的吞吐量而非准确率；（2）高效计算型工作负载，在规模化运行时遭受SMT争用；（3）内存带宽受限型工作负载，即使单副本运行时L3过滤效果也较差。发现仅在系统满载时才出现的规模化效应，包括SMT调度争用导致的吞吐量下降和L3容量干扰。

Conclusion: 该工作为下一代数据中心处理器的架构研究和工作负载驱动设计决策建立了实证基础，强调了在基准测试特征分析中考虑规模化效应的重要性，这些效应在传统单副本分析中是不可见的。

Abstract: SPEC CPU 2026 is the first major update to the industry-standard CPU benchmark suite since 2017. This paper presents the first microarchitecture based performance characterization of the new suite, conducted on AMD EPYC "Zen 5", also the first SPEC CPU characterization study on this microarchitecture. Using a multi-lens methodology spanning pipeline efficiency, control flow behavior, cache hierarchy pressure, and instruction mix, we analyze both SPECrate and SPECspeed suites. We introduce scale analysis, comparing single-copy to full-system behavior to expose system-level bottlenecks invisible to conventional characterization. Our analysis reveals substantial behavioral diversity across the suite, and the multi-lens analysis identifies three distinct behavioral clusters: frontend control-flow-dominated workloads that stress branch predictor throughput rather than accuracy, high-efficiency compute workloads that suffer SMT contention at scale, and memory bandwidth-bound workloads with poor L3 filtering even at single-copy. Scale-dependent effects, including SMT dispatch contention causing throughput reduction and L3 capacity interference, emerge only at full system utilization. This work establishes an empirical foundation for architectural research and workload-driven design decisions targeting next-generation datacenter processors.

</details>
